Company brief

RENTOX AS is a private limited company operating in Annen rengjøringsvirksomhet og snømåking. Latest accounts (2025): revenue NOK 15.4m, annual result NOK 1.8m. Annual accounts available for 2020-2025 (6 years). Detailed records only available as PDFs. Revenue shrinking (-17.8% YoY).
